{
  "server": "westd-32570",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cat > /tmp/batch_export.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport os, json, base64, sys\n\nbatch_idx = int(sys.argv[1])\nmax_batch_bytes = 5500  # stay well under SSH limit\n\nfiles = []\nfor root, dirs, fnames in os.walk('/tmp/backup_extract/results'):\n    for f in fnames:\n        if f.endswith('.json'):\n            path = os.path.join(root, f)\n            rel = os.path.relpath(path, '/tmp/backup_extract')\n            size = os.path.getsize(path)\n            files.append((rel, path, size))\nfiles.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n# Group into batches\nbatches = []\ncurrent_batch = []\ncurrent_size = 0\nfor rel, path, size in files:\n    if size > max_batch_bytes:\n        # large files get their own batch(es) with chunking\n        if current_batch:\n            batches.append(('files', current_batch))\n            current_batch = []\n            current_size = 0\n        # Split large file into chunks\n        with open(path, 'r') as fh:\n            content = fh.read()\n        chunk_size = max_batch_bytes\n        chunks = [content[i:i+chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(content), chunk_size)]\n        for ci, chunk in enumerate(chunks):\n            batches.append(('chunk', rel, ci, len(chunks), chunk))\n    else:\n        if current_size + size > max_batch_bytes and current_batch:\n            batches.append(('files', current_batch))\n            current_batch = []\n            current_size = 0\n        current_batch.append((rel, path))\n        current_size += size\nif current_batch:\n    batches.append(('files', current_batch))\n\nif batch_idx == -1:\n    print(f'TOTAL_BATCHES:{len(batches)}')\n    for i, b in enumerate(batches):\n        if b[0] == 'files':\n            names = [os.path.basename(x[0]) for x in b[1]]\n            print(f'  {i}: files({len(b[1])}): {\", \".join(names)}')\n        else:\n            print(f'  {i}: chunk {b[2]+1}/{b[3]} of {b[1]}')\n    sys.exit(0)\n\nif batch_idx >= len(batches):\n    print('DONE')\n    sys.exit(0)\n\nbatch = batches[batch_idx]\nif batch[0] == 'files':\n    result = {}\n    for rel, path in batch[1]:\n        with open(path, 'r') as fh:\n            result[rel] = fh.read()\n    # Output as: type=files, then JSON\n    print('TYPE:files')\n    print(json.dumps(result, separators=(',', ':')))\nelse:\n    _, rel, ci, total, chunk = batch\n    print(f'TYPE:chunk')\n    print(f'FILE:{rel}')\n    print(f'PART:{ci}/{total}')\n    print(chunk)\nPYEOF\necho \"Script written\"\n"
}

{
  "server": "westd-32570",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 -c \"\nimport json, os\n\nbase = '/tmp/backup_extract/results'\n\n# Mapping from server path to local subdir\nmapping = {\n    'analysis/': 'analysis_json/',\n    'exp004_8model_analysis/forward_prediction/': 'exp004_json/forward_prediction/',\n    'exp004_8model_analysis/': 'exp004_json/',\n    'exp003_': 'exp003_json/',\n    'partial_credit_multimodel/': 'analysis_json/',\n    'exp001_analysis/': 'analysis_json/',\n    'exp004_ff_residual/': 'exp004_json/',\n    'sanity_check.json': 'analysis_json/',\n    'exp001_bf16_2temp_comparison.json': 'analysis_json/',\n}\n\n# Get all files sorted by size\nfiles = []\nfor root, dirs, fnames in os.walk(base):\n    for fn in fnames:\n        if fn.endswith('.json'):\n            full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            relpath = os.path.relpath(full, base)\n            size = os.path.getsize(full)\n            # Determine local subdir\n            local_dir = None\n            for prefix, ldir in mapping.items():\n                if relpath.startswith(prefix):\n                    local_dir = ldir\n                    break\n            files.append((size, relpath, local_dir, fn))\n\nfiles.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\nfor s, r, ld, fn in files:\n    print(f'{s}\\t{ld}\\t{fn}\\t{r}')\n\""
}

{
  "server": "westd-32570",
  "command": "cat > /tmp/serve_batch.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport json, os, sys\n\nbase = '/tmp/backup_extract/results'\nbatch_num = int(sys.argv[1])\nmax_bytes = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 5000\n\n# Collect all files sorted by size\nfiles = []\nfor root, dirs, fnames in os.walk(base):\n    for fn in fnames:\n        if fn.endswith('.json'):\n            full = os.path.join(root, fn)\n            relpath = os.path.relpath(full, base)\n            size = os.path.getsize(full)\n            files.append((size, relpath, full))\nfiles.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n# Group into batches\nbatches = []\ncurrent_batch = []\ncurrent_size = 0\nfor size, relpath, full in files:\n    if size > max_bytes:\n        # Large files get their own batch\n        if current_batch:\n            batches.append(current_batch)\n            current_batch = []\n            current_size = 0\n        batches.append([(size, relpath, full)])\n        continue\n    if current_size + size > max_bytes and current_batch:\n        batches.append(current_batch)\n        current_batch = []\n        current_size = 0\n    current_batch.append((size, relpath, full))\n    current_size += size\nif current_batch:\n    batches.append(current_batch)\n\nif batch_num == -1:\n    # Print batch info\n    for i, batch in enumerate(batches):\n        total = sum(s for s, _, _ in batch)\n        names = [r for _, r, _ in batch]\n        print(f\"Batch {i}: {len(batch)} files, {total} bytes: {', '.join(names)}\")\n    print(f\"Total: {len(batches)} batches, {len(files)} files\")\nelse:\n    if batch_num >= len(batches):\n        print(json.dumps({\"error\": f\"batch {batch_num} >= {len(batches)}\"}))\n        sys.exit(0)\n    batch = batches[batch_num]\n    result = {}\n    for size, relpath, full in batch:\n        with open(full, 'r') as f:\n            result[relpath] = f.read()\n    print(json.dumps(result))\nPYEOF\necho \"Script written\""
}

1	"""Project secrets vault — encrypted per-project key storage with usage approval.
2	
3	Director stores secrets (API keys, tokens, passwords). Agent requests usage
4	via Feishu approval card — value is returned only after user approves.
5	
6	Storage layout:
7	  config/secrets/{project}/manifest.yaml   — key metadata (no values)
8	  config/secrets/{project}/{key_id}.enc    — encrypted key value
9	"""
10	
11	from __future__ import annotations
12	
13	import logging
14	from datetime import datetime, timezone
15	from pathlib import Path
16	
17	import yaml
18	
19	from core import paths as _paths
20	from core.crypto import save_secret, load_secret
21	from core.tools.result import ToolResult
22	
23	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
24	
25	
26	def _secrets_dir(project: str) -> Path:
27	    return _paths.config_dir() / "secrets" / project
28	
29	
30	def _manifest_path(project: str) -> Path:
31	    return _secrets_dir(project) / "manifest.yaml"
32	
33	
34	def _load_manifest(project: str) -> dict:
35	    path = _manifest_path(project)
36	    if not path.exists():
37	        return {}
38	    with open(path) as f:
39	        return yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
40	
41	
42	def _save_manifest(project: str, data: dict) -> None:
43	    path = _manifest_path(project)
44	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
45	    with open(path, "w") as f:
46	        yaml.dump(data, f, allow_unicode=True, default_flow_style=False)
47	
48	
49	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
50	# Public API
51	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

1	"""Symmetric encryption for secrets (Admin Tokens, API Keys).
2	
3	Uses Fernet (AES-128-CBC + HMAC-SHA256) with a key derived from the machine ID.
4	Goal: prevent plain-text exposure if files are read by the Agent or leaked —
5	this is NOT a substitute for OS-level access control.
6	"""
7	
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	import base64
11	import hashlib
12	import uuid
13	from pathlib import Path
14	
15	from cryptography.fernet import Fernet, InvalidToken
16	
17	# Deterministic salt — changing this invalidates all stored secrets.
18	_SALT = b"agent-ml-research-tum-v1"
19	
20	
21	def _derive_key() -> bytes:
22	    """Derive a Fernet key from the machine's MAC address + salt."""
23	    machine_id = str(uuid.getnode()).encode()
24	    raw = hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac("sha256", machine_id, _SALT, iterations=100_000, dklen=32)
25	    return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(raw)
26	
27	
28	_fernet: Fernet | None = None
29	
30	
31	def _get_fernet() -> Fernet:
32	    global _fernet
33	    if _fernet is None:
34	        _fernet = Fernet(_derive_key())
35	    return _fernet
36	
37	
38	def encrypt(plaintext: str) -> str:
39	    """Encrypt a string, return URL-safe base64 ciphertext."""
40	    return _get_fernet().encrypt(plaintext.encode()).decode()
41	
42	
43	def decrypt(ciphertext: str) -> str | None:
44	    """Decrypt ciphertext. Returns None if invalid/corrupted."""
45	    try:
46	        return _get_fernet().decrypt(ciphertext.encode()).decode()
47	    except (InvalidToken, Exception):
48	        return None
49	
50	
51	# --- File-level helpers (for admin token / API keys) ---
52	
53	def save_secret(path: Path, plaintext: str) -> None:
54	    """Encrypt and write a secret to a file."""
55	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
56	    path.write_text(encrypt(plaintext))
57	
58	
59	def load_secret(path: Path) -> str | None:
60	    """Read and decrypt a secret from a file. Returns None if missing/invalid."""
61	    if not path.exists():
62	        return None
63	    ciphertext = path.read_text().strip()
64	    if not ciphertext:
65	        return None
66	    return decrypt(ciphertext)
67
